# CommodoreSQL data dictionary

This generated dictionary describes every relation and relation column declared in the
canonical `schema.dbml`. It intentionally does not query the live database. Field-level
source, format, population, and NULL contracts are resolved deterministically from SQL-aligned
rules, DBML notes, and the maintained Master Section appendix. Regenerate it with
`python3 scripts/generate_data_dictionary.py`.

View columns are included, including complete inherited schemas and field metadata for true
`SELECT *` projections. Inherited rows also identify the view filter that changes population.

## Source ownership and compatibility naming

BMG owns course-materials and raw pricing/cost observations; BVA owns opt-out and
mailing-history inputs; IPEDS owns institution metadata. Existing executable source-table
names are compatibility names rather than source-prefixed names. Derived outputs omit
source acronyms.

## Non-Master enrichment NULL semantics

- Nullable IPEDS descriptors mean no matching institution metadata was available.
- A NULL panel response year means no recorded response year was available from the BVA
  panel input. `is_opted_out=FALSE` is the executable default when no cleaned email matches
  the BVA opt-out input; the opt-out flag itself is not nullable.
- Nullable pricing fields after a LEFT join mean no exact matched value or no valid price;
  `has_pricing_match` separates an unmatched row from a matched row with NULL price cells.
- `current_mailing_other` is the complete residual after normalized CA, TX, FL, NY, PA,
  and CAN routing, including NULL, blank, and unknown state values.

### `panel_email`

- Kind: table
- Stage: IMPORT / 0_setup.sql
- Grain: One cleaned email
- Upstream: `panel`
- Intended use: Deduplicated panel enrichment that preserves multiplicity audits.
- DBML relation note: Materialized by 0_setup.sql. One row per cleaned email, with latest response year and source multiplicity counts; prevents panel-history joins from multiplying comprehensive_data.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `email` | `varchar` | Group key from normalized `panel.email`. | Lowercase, trimmed email text | One cleaned panel email. | Not expected; NULL emails group together if present in source. |
| `panel_response_year` | `varchar` | `MAX(panel.response_year)`. | Panel response year text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | All retained panel-history rows for the cleaned email. | No nonmissing response year for the email. |
| `panel_source_row_count` | `bigint` | `COUNT(*)` of retained panel rows. | Non-negative whole-number count | All retained panel-history rows for the cleaned email. | Never NULL; at least one. |
| `panel_response_year_variant_count` | `bigint` | `COUNT(DISTINCT panel.response_year)`. | Non-negative whole-number count | All retained panel-history rows for the cleaned email. | Never NULL; zero means all years missing. |

### `pricing_historical`

- Kind: table
- Stage: IMPORT step 3 / 1_bookprices_import.sql
- Grain: One latest section × ISBN × option × condition × format × rental-term row
- Upstream: `BMG BookPricing.Historical_20260224.csv`
- Intended use: Deduplicated BMG-owned raw pricing/cost history in a compatibility-named table.
- DBML relation note: ~30M rows. BMG-owned raw pricing/cost snapshot in a compatibility-named table after import/dedupe. Latest pricing_date is retained at (section_id, isbn13, book_option, book_condition, book_format, rental_days), instructor names are aggregated, and indexes remain on section_id, isbn13, and period_sortable. Catalog classification is never written back.

### `supply_isbn_classification`

- Kind: table
- Stage: IMPORT derived / 1a_supply_classification.sql
- Grain: One classified ISBN
- Upstream: `course_catalog_20251215`, `supply_keywords.tsv`
- Intended use: Precision-oriented supply classification audit.
- DBML relation note: Created by 1a_supply_classification.sql. One row per classified ISBN. A title variant qualifies when lowercase substring matching finds an include pattern and no exclude pattern; any qualifying 2024+ title variant can place the ISBN in this table. Attribution prefers specific rules over generic >supply</>suppy< fallbacks, then longest-pattern and stable lexical tie-breaks; this does not change Use/NoUse membership.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `isbn13` | `bigint` | Non-NULL catalog ISBN group key after qualifying title-pattern matches. | logical unique key; non-NULL classified ISBN from the catalog source; ISBN or source identifier/pseudo-SKU represented in the declared relation type | One classified non-NULL ISBN in the 2024+ catalog. | Not produced. |
| `title` | `varchar` | Deterministic representative catalog title for the ISBN. | representative matched catalog title | One classified non-NULL ISBN in the 2024+ catalog. | No representative nonmissing title. |
| `n_rows` | `hugeint` | Count of 2024+ catalog rows covered by qualifying title variants. | 2024+ catalog rows covered by qualifying title variants | One classified non-NULL ISBN in the 2024+ catalog. | Not produced. |
| `matched_pattern` | `varchar` | Highest-priority included lowercase keyword; longest then lexical tie-break. | representative lowercase include-pattern match: specific rules before generic supply markers, then longest and lexical tie-breaks | One classified non-NULL ISBN in the 2024+ catalog. | Not produced. |
| `category` | `varchar` | Category attached to the selected supply keyword. | category associated with matched_pattern | One classified non-NULL ISBN in the 2024+ catalog. | Not produced. |

### `section_book_status`

- Kind: table
- Stage: IMPORT derived / 1b_section_filter.sql
- Grain: One period-specific section_id
- Upstream: `course_catalog_20251215`, `supply_isbn_classification`
- Intended use: Supply-aware required-status fallback lookup.
- DBML relation note: One row per section_id. Drives catalog-side is_required_inferred logic in comprehensive_data only. has_required is supply-aware (#40); built in 1a_/1b_ after supply_isbn_classification.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `section_id` | `varchar` | Group key from `course_catalog_20251215.section_id`. | `course_id::section-code::period_sortable` composite | All catalog rows for the period-specific section. | Not produced. |
| `has_required` | `boolean` | `BOOL_OR(book_status='required' AND NOT is_supply)` over catalog rows. | #40 supply-aware: TRUE if section has any required-status NON-SUPPLY material (BOOL_OR(book_status=required AND NOT is_supply)). A supply-only required item no longer forces TRUE, so a co-listed blank-status real textbook keeps the required fallback. | All catalog rows for the period-specific section. | Never NULL; false means no nonsupply required-status row. |

### `pricing_wide`

- Kind: table
- Stage: IMPORT derived / 2c_pricing_wide.sql
- Grain: One section_id × ISBN13
- Upstream: `pricing_historical`
- Intended use: BMG-owned wide price and availability pivot.
- DBML relation note: TABLE (materialized). One row per (section_id × isbn13) over all BMG-owned pricing_historical rows. Retains source/provenance descriptors and derives 18 option × condition × format price cells plus price/availability aggregates. price_buy_min/max feed the "owned" cost columns. It contains no is_required_inferred, OER/IA, or IPEDS/catalog enrichment, and no required-only pricing view is created.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `section_id` | `varchar` | Group key from `pricing_historical.section_id`. | `course_id::section-code::period_sortable` composite | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | Not produced for a retained group. |
| `isbn13` | `varchar` | Group key from `pricing_historical.isbn13`. | ISBN or source identifier/pseudo-SKU represented in the declared relation type | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | Not produced for a retained group. |
| `unit_id` | `bigint` | `MAX(pricing_historical.unit_id)` for source provenance/metadata. | source provenance: MAX(unit_id) over the group (constant per source section) | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | No nonmissing value in the group. |
| `required` | `boolean` | `BOOL_OR(pricing_historical.required)`. | BOOL_OR(source-derived required) over the (section, isbn) group | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | NULL when every retained row has required = NULL (for example, Book Status is missing); otherwise TRUE if any row is required and FALSE when all non-NULL values are false. |
| `institute` | `varchar` | `MAX(pricing_historical.institute)` for source provenance/metadata. | Institute text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | No nonmissing value in the group. |
| `bookstore_url` | `varchar` | `MAX(pricing_historical.bookstore_url)` for source provenance/metadata. | Bookstore url text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | No nonmissing value in the group. |
| `title` | `varchar` | `MAX(pricing_historical.title)` for source provenance/metadata. | Title text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | No nonmissing value in the group. |
| `author` | `varchar` | `MAX(pricing_historical.author)` for source provenance/metadata. | Author text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | No nonmissing value in the group. |

| `edition` | `varchar` | `MAX(pricing_historical.edition)` for source provenance/metadata. | Edition text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | No nonmissing value in the group. |

| `price_max` | `decimal(10,2)` | `MAX(price)` after prices >=9999 are normalized to NULL; zero is retained. | USD decimal at the precision declared in DBML | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | No price below the 9999 sentinel ceiling in the group. |
| `price_avg` | `double` | Legacy midpoint `(price_min + price_max) / 2.0`; not AVG(). | LEGACY definition: (price_min + price_max) / 2 — NOT the arithmetic mean. See CLAUDE.md. | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | Either price bound is missing. |
| `rental_days_min` | `integer` | Minimum nonmissing rental term. | shortest rental term offered for this (section, isbn) | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | No listing has an explicit rental term. |
| `rental_days_max` | `integer` | Maximum nonmissing rental term. | longest rental term offered (~95 distinct values, summarized as range) | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | No listing has an explicit rental term. |
| `price_buy_min` | `decimal(10,2)` | `MIN(price)` FILTERed to buy after prices >=9999 become NULL; rentals excluded. | MIN buy-option price (owned; rentals excluded) — feeds owned cost | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | No buy price below the 9999 sentinel ceiling. |
| `price_buy_max` | `decimal(10,2)` | `MAX(price)` FILTERed to buy after prices >=9999 become NULL; rentals excluded. | MAX buy-option price (owned; rentals excluded) | All retained pricing observations for one `(section_id, isbn13)` group. | No buy price below the 9999 sentinel ceiling. |

- Kind: table
- Stage: Canonical materials / 2b_course_materials.sql
- Grain: One period × section × ISBN, plus one NULL-ISBN audit row per section when present

Built from comprehensive_data and section_enrollment; retains source/variant/conflict evidence and canonical population flags. The validated source_row_count sum is 102,885,609.

### `section_enrollment`

- Kind: table
- Stage: Canonical sections / 2b_course_materials.sql
- Grain: One valid 2024+ period × section
- Upstream: `comprehensive_data`
- Intended use: Complete section population and authoritative enrollment assignment.
- DBML relation note: Created by 2b_course_materials.sql. Materialized one row per valid 2024+ (period_sortable, section_id), currently 23,580,550 rows. Owns the complete section population, authoritative raw enrollment/flags, section-scope dimensions, and assigned-enrollment value/provenance. course_materials and material_costs consume its section fields; master_section joins the retained material-bearing subset.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `section_id` | `varchar` | Group key from `comprehensive_data.section_id`. | period-specific section key; period_sortable is embedded in the key | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | Missing upstream descriptor; section and sortable-period keys are required. |
| `course_id` | `varchar` | Deterministic section value (`any_value` or lexical `mode` as defined in sql) from `comprehensive_data.course_id`. | `unit_id::department-code::course-number` composite | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | Missing upstream descriptor; section and sortable-period keys are required. |
| `period_sortable` | `varchar` | Deterministic section value (`any_value` or lexical `mode` as defined in sql) from `comprehensive_data.period_sortable`. | YYYY-N | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | Missing upstream descriptor; section and sortable-period keys are required. |
| `control` | `varchar` | Deterministic section value (`any_value` or lexical `mode` as defined in sql) from `comprehensive_data.control`. | section-canonical scope dimension reused by material_costs | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | Missing upstream descriptor; section and sortable-period keys are required. |
| `level` | `varchar` | Deterministic section value (`any_value` or lexical `mode` as defined in sql) from `comprehensive_data.level`. | section-canonical scope dimension reused by material_costs | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | Missing upstream descriptor; section and sortable-period keys are required. |
| `sector` | `varchar` | Deterministic section value (`any_value` or lexical `mode` as defined in sql) from `comprehensive_data.sector`. | section-canonical scope dimension reused by material_costs | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | Missing upstream descriptor; section and sortable-period keys are required. |
| `course_level` | `varchar` | Deterministic section value (`any_value` or lexical `mode` as defined in sql) from `comprehensive_data.course_level`. | section mode with lexical tie-break; reused by material_costs for section-scope consistency | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | Missing upstream descriptor; section and sortable-period keys are required. |
| `enrollments` | `integer` | `MAX(comprehensive_data.enrollments)` for the section; raw 9999 seats sentinel retained. | raw catalog enrollment; never overwritten | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | No reported enrollments. |
| `seats_taken` | `integer` | `MAX(comprehensive_data.seats_taken)` for the section; raw 9999 seats sentinel retained. | raw catalog seats; 9999 is invalid | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | No reported seats taken. |
| `has_enrollment` | `boolean` | Section max(enrollments) is non-null. | TRUE or FALSE | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | Never NULL; false means the signal is absent. |
| `has_enrollment_sibling` | `boolean` | Another same-course/same-term section has enrollment. | TRUE or FALSE | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | Never NULL; false means the signal is absent. |
| `has_enrollment_own_seats` | `boolean` | Section max(seats_taken) is non-null and below 9999. | TRUE or FALSE | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | Never NULL; false means the signal is absent. |
| `has_enrollment_sibling_seats` | `boolean` | Another same-course/same-term section has valid seats. | TRUE or FALSE | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | Never NULL; false means the signal is absent. |
| `enrollment_assigned` | `integer` | Rounded first available: own enrollment, own valid seats, course enrollment median, course seats median, control×level median, level median. | Exact section enrollment after the documented own/seats/sibling/median hierarchy; NULL only when no rung is available | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | No assignment ladder rung produced a value (`enrollment_source='none'`). |
| `enrollment_source` | `varchar` | Label for the first successful enrollment-assignment ladder rung. | own \| own_seats \| sibling_enroll \| sibling_seats \| class_median \| level_median \| none | All valid 2024+ catalog rows for one period-specific section; independent of material inclusion. | Never NULL; `none` means unassigned. |

### `master_mailing`

- Kind: table
- Stage: EDA mailing / 3_mailing_lists.sql
- Grain: One non-NULL, nonblank cleaned email
- Upstream: `course_catalog_20251215`
- Intended use: Persisted canonical deterministic mailing selection; independent of opt-out and panel history.
- DBML relation note: Persisted canonical mailing selection. One deterministically selected row per non-NULL, nonblank cleaned email directly from course_catalog_20251215; newest period, largest enrollment, then stable source-field ordering. Opt-out and panel history do not affect selection.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `unit_id` | `bigint` | Deterministically selected `course_catalog_20251215.unit_id` directly from `${SURVEY_TABLE}` for each cleaned email. | IPEDS institution identifier | All normalized catalog rows sharing one non-NULL, nonblank cleaned email; opt_out and panel history do not participate. | Selected source row has no value for this field. |
| `school` | `varchar` | Deterministically selected `course_catalog_20251215.school` directly from `${SURVEY_TABLE}` for each cleaned email. | School text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | All normalized catalog rows sharing one non-NULL, nonblank cleaned email; opt_out and panel history do not participate. | Selected source row has no value for this field. |
| `state` | `varchar` | Deterministically selected `course_catalog_20251215.state` directly from `${SURVEY_TABLE}` for each cleaned email. | State text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | All normalized catalog rows sharing one non-NULL, nonblank cleaned email; opt_out and panel history do not participate. | Selected source row has no value for this field. |
| `department` | `varchar` | Deterministically selected `course_catalog_20251215.department` directly from `${SURVEY_TABLE}` for each cleaned email. | Department text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | All normalized catalog rows sharing one non-NULL, nonblank cleaned email; opt_out and panel history do not participate. | Selected source row has no value for this field. |
| `course_level` | `varchar` | Deterministically selected `course_catalog_20251215.course_level` directly from `${SURVEY_TABLE}` for each cleaned email. | Course level text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | All normalized catalog rows sharing one non-NULL, nonblank cleaned email; opt_out and panel history do not participate. | Selected source row has no value for this field. |
| `course_subject` | `varchar` | Deterministically selected `course_catalog_20251215.course_subject` directly from `${SURVEY_TABLE}` for each cleaned email. | Course subject text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | All normalized catalog rows sharing one non-NULL, nonblank cleaned email; opt_out and panel history do not participate. | Selected source row has no value for this field. |
| `period` | `varchar` | Deterministically selected `course_catalog_20251215.period` directly from `${SURVEY_TABLE}` for each cleaned email. | Academic term label such as `Fall 2024` | All normalized catalog rows sharing one non-NULL, nonblank cleaned email; opt_out and panel history do not participate. | Selected source row has no value for this field. |

### `recent_periods`

- Kind: view
- Stage: EDA mailing / 3_mailing_lists.sql
- Grain: One of the latest 12 distinct non-NULL periods
- Upstream: `master_mailing`
- Intended use: Period boundary used by current_mailing.
- Projection: Declared one-column projection from persisted master_mailing.
- DBML relation note: Newest 12 distinct non-NULL period_sortable values present in persisted master_mailing; bounds current_mailing without rescanning the catalog source.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `period_sortable` | `varchar` | Latest 12 distinct nonmissing `master_mailing.period_sortable` values. | YYYY-N | One retained recent term boundary from the persisted master selection. | Not produced. |

### `current_mailing`

- Kind: view
- Stage: EDA mailing / 3_mailing_lists.sql
- Grain: One non-opted-out cleaned email selected in the latest 12 master periods
- Upstream: `master_mailing`, `recent_periods`, `panel_email`, `opt_out`
- Intended use: Whiteboard Mailing Working view with panel response enrichment.
- Projection: Master fields pass through; panel_response_year is LEFT-joined; recent-period and opt-out filters define population.
- DBML relation note: Whiteboard Mailing Working view. Recent master_mailing rows excluding any email present in opt_out, LEFT-enriched with panel_response_year from panel_email.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `unit_id` | `bigint` | Passthrough from `master_mailing.unit_id` after membership in `recent_periods` and `NOT EXISTS` exclusion by `opt_out.email`. | IPEDS institution identifier | Master-selected emails in the latest 12 selected periods that have no opt_out match. | Selected master row has no value for this field. |
| `school` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `master_mailing.school` after membership in `recent_periods` and `NOT EXISTS` exclusion by `opt_out.email`. | School text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Master-selected emails in the latest 12 selected periods that have no opt_out match. | Selected master row has no value for this field. |
| `state` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `master_mailing.state` after membership in `recent_periods` and `NOT EXISTS` exclusion by `opt_out.email`. | State text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Master-selected emails in the latest 12 selected periods that have no opt_out match. | Selected master row has no value for this field. |
| `department` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `master_mailing.department` after membership in `recent_periods` and `NOT EXISTS` exclusion by `opt_out.email`. | Department text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Master-selected emails in the latest 12 selected periods that have no opt_out match. | Selected master row has no value for this field. |
| `course_level` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `master_mailing.course_level` after membership in `recent_periods` and `NOT EXISTS` exclusion by `opt_out.email`. | Course level text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Master-selected emails in the latest 12 selected periods that have no opt_out match. | Selected master row has no value for this field. |
| `course_subject` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `master_mailing.course_subject` after membership in `recent_periods` and `NOT EXISTS` exclusion by `opt_out.email`. | Course subject text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Master-selected emails in the latest 12 selected periods that have no opt_out match. | Selected master row has no value for this field. |
| `period` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `master_mailing.period` after membership in `recent_periods` and `NOT EXISTS` exclusion by `opt_out.email`. | Academic term label such as `Fall 2024` | Master-selected emails in the latest 12 selected periods that have no opt_out match. | Selected master row has no value for this field. |
| `period_sortable` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `master_mailing.period_sortable` after membership in `recent_periods` and `NOT EXISTS` exclusion by `opt_out.email`. | `YYYY-N`; 1=Winter, 2=Spring, 3=Summer, 4=Fall | Master-selected emails in the latest 12 selected periods that have no opt_out match. | Selected master row has no value for this field. |
| `period_date` | `date` | Passthrough from `master_mailing.period_date` after membership in `recent_periods` and `NOT EXISTS` exclusion by `opt_out.email`. | Canonical date: YYYY-01-01, YYYY-04-01, YYYY-07-01, or YYYY-10-01 | Master-selected emails in the latest 12 selected periods that have no opt_out match. | Selected master row has no value for this field. |
| `instructor` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `master_mailing.instructor` after membership in `recent_periods` and `NOT EXISTS` exclusion by `opt_out.email`. | Instructor text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Master-selected emails in the latest 12 selected periods that have no opt_out match. | Selected master row has no value for this field. |
| `first_name` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `master_mailing.first_name` after membership in `recent_periods` and `NOT EXISTS` exclusion by `opt_out.email`. | First name text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Master-selected emails in the latest 12 selected periods that have no opt_out match. | Selected master row has no value for this field. |
| `last_name` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `master_mailing.last_name` after membership in `recent_periods` and `NOT EXISTS` exclusion by `opt_out.email`. | Last name text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Master-selected emails in the latest 12 selected periods that have no opt_out match. | Selected master row has no value for this field. |
| `email` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `master_mailing.email` after membership in `recent_periods` and `NOT EXISTS` exclusion by `opt_out.email`. | Lowercase, trimmed email text | Master-selected emails in the latest 12 selected periods that have no opt_out match. | Not produced; retained current emails are required. |
| `panel_response_year` | `varchar` | `panel_email.panel_response_year` LEFT JOINed to `master_mailing` by cleaned email. | Panel response year text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Master-selected emails in recent_periods with no opt_out match. | No recorded panel response year for the email. |

### `material_costs`

- Kind: table
- Stage: EDA records / 3b_material_costs.sql
- Grain: One canonical Use period × section × ISBN item
- Upstream: `course_materials_use`, `pricing_wide`
- Intended use: Approved item-level input with optional LEFT pricing enrichment.
- DBML relation note: Created by 3b_material_costs.sql. TABLE (materialized), one row per (period_sortable, section_id, isbn13) canonical Use item. Built from course_materials_use and LEFT-enriched from source-owned pricing_wide on the current exact section × ISBN join, so items without usable prices remain. Catalog attributes are never sourced from pricing. The 17 canonical panel/audit fields above append after has_pricing_match (129 total columns). Established current baseline: 12,806,060 rows. Detailed matching evidence is centralized in CMM-ETL.md issue #21 limitation.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `isbn13` | `bigint` | Passthrough from `course_materials_use.isbn13`. | canonical numeric ISBN13; NULL only for audit rows; ISBN or source identifier/pseudo-SKU represented in the declared relation type | Canonical Use section×ISBN items retained by `material_costs`. | ISBN is NULL only on audit groups; section and sortable-period keys are required. |
| `book_title` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `course_materials_use.book_title`. | Book title text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Canonical Use section×ISBN items retained by `material_costs`. | No grouped source row has a nonmissing representative value. |
| `author` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `course_materials_use.author`. | Author text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Canonical Use section×ISBN items retained by `material_costs`. | No grouped source row has a nonmissing representative value. |
| `publisher` | `varchar` | Passthrough from `course_materials_use.publisher`. | Publisher text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Canonical Use section×ISBN items retained by `material_costs`. | No grouped source row has a nonmissing representative value. |

### `section_cost`

- Kind: table
- Stage: EDA records / 4_merged_records.sql
- Grain: One material-bearing period × section
- Upstream: `material_costs`
- Intended use: Section-level price-bound aggregates used by release rollups.
- DBML relation note: TABLE. One row per (period_sortable, section_id) represented by canonical material_costs. Aggregates section-term-ISBN items, required vs non-required via is_required_inferred; owned = buy-only. Joins 1:1 into the material-bearing master_section and feeds master_course cost rollup; it is not the approved item-level input.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

### `master_section`

- Kind: table
- Stage: EDA records / 4_merged_records.sql
- Grain: One material-bearing period × section
- Upstream: `material_costs`, `section_cost`, `section_enrollment`, `course_materials`
- Intended use: Canonical materialized per-term section release table.
- DBML relation note: TABLE (materialized), currently 6,983,049 rows. Exactly one row per distinct (period_sortable, section_id) in material_costs. Material/OER/coverage/publisher fields aggregate canonical material_costs items; price/cost fields join from section_cost; section dimensions and assigned enrollment join from section_enrollment. Supply, NoUse, Canada, and placeholder fields are course_materials sidecar audits limited to retained material-bearing sections. Validated post-#65 canonical sidecar totals: NoUse 78,230; no_details 25,993; no_materials 145; supply 52,237. Complete-population denominators remain in section_enrollment. DATA-DICTIONARY.md is the authoritative full column dictionary; its embedded MASTER-SECTION-DICTIONARY.md appendix owns the detailed business/NULL/denominator contract.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `section_id` | `varchar` | Group key from `material_costs`; includes term | `course_id::section-code::period_sortable` composite | Material-section population | Not produced |
| `course_id` | `varchar` | Exact section value from `section_enrollment`; source composite omits section and term | `unit_id::department-code::course-number` composite | Material-section population | Not expected; missing source segments are encoded as `UNKNOWN` |
| `period` | `varchar` | `ANY_VALUE(period)` from `material_costs` | Academic term label such as `Fall 2024` | Material-section population | Not expected because valid sortable periods are required |
| `period_sortable` | `varchar` | Group key from `material_costs`; `YYYY-N` | `YYYY-N`; 1=Winter, 2=Spring, 3=Summer, 4=Fall | Material-section population | Not produced |
| `period_date` | `date` | `ANY_VALUE(period_date)` from `material_costs` | Canonical date: YYYY-01-01, YYYY-04-01, YYYY-07-01, or YYYY-10-01 | Material-section population, restricted to 2024+ | Not produced by the retained scope |
| `unit_id` | `bigint` | `ANY_VALUE(unit_id)` from `material_costs` | IPEDS institution identifier | Material-section population | Source institution ID missing |
| `state` | `varchar` | `ANY_VALUE(state)` from `material_costs` | State text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Material-section population | No source geography |
| `control` | `varchar` | Exact section value from `section_enrollment` | Control text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Material-section population | No matching IPEDS institution |

| `size` | `varchar` | `ANY_VALUE(size)` from `material_costs` | Size text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Material-section population | No matching IPEDS institution |

| `enrollment_2024` | `integer` | `ANY_VALUE(enrollment_2024)` from `material_costs` | Whole number in the declared integer range | Material-section population | No matching IPEDS value |
| `distance_enrollment_2024` | `integer` | `ANY_VALUE(distance_enrollment_2024)` from `material_costs` | Whole number in the declared integer range | Material-section population | No matching IPEDS value |
| `school` | `varchar` | Most-frequent non-NULL `material_costs` value via `mode()` | School text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Canonical items in the section | No non-NULL source value |
| `department` | `varchar` | Most-frequent non-NULL `material_costs` value via `mode()` | Department text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Canonical items in the section | No non-NULL source value |

| `course_title` | `varchar` | Most-frequent non-NULL `material_costs` value via `mode()` | Course title text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Canonical items in the section | No non-NULL source value |

| `material_count` | `bigint` | `COUNT(*)` over deduplicated `material_costs` | COUNT of canonical material_costs section×ISBN items; always >0 | Canonical section×ISBN items | Never NULL or zero |
| `required_count` | `bigint` | Count of `material_costs` rows where `is_required_inferred` | COUNT of material_costs items WHERE is_required_inferred; A/B split key | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `optional_count` | `bigint` | Count of `material_costs` rows where not `is_required_inferred` | COUNT of material_costs items WHERE NOT is_required_inferred | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `has_course_material_use` | `boolean` | Constant true for a retained material section | Always true: membership requires at least one canonical Use item | Material-section population | Never NULL or false |
| `course_material_use_count` | `bigint` | Count of canonical items; equals `material_count` | Canonical material items; equals material_count | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL or zero |
| `course_material_no_use_count` | `bigint` | Count of `course_materials.is_course_material_no_use` rows | Co-occurring NoUse catalog rows for retained sections only; sidecar audit | Canonical rows for retained material sections only | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `no_details_count` | `bigint` | Count of exact `*No Book Details*` rows in the sidecar | Co-occurring no_details rows for retained sections only; sidecar audit | Canonical `course_materials` rows for retained material sections only; reasons may overlap | Never NULL; zero means marker absent |
| `no_materials_count` | `bigint` | Count of exact `*No Books Required*` or `placeholder_no_material` rows in the sidecar | Co-occurring no_materials rows for retained sections only; sidecar audit | Canonical `course_materials` rows for retained material sections only; reasons may overlap | Never NULL; zero means marker absent |
| `is_canada` | `boolean` | `BOOL_OR(state='CAN')` in the sidecar | Co-occurring Canada row for a retained section; sidecar audit, not a full-population indicator | Canonical `course_materials` rows for retained material sections only | Never NULL; false means no Canadian row |
| `is_supply` | `boolean` | `BOOL_OR(is_supply)` in the sidecar | #36: co-occurring classified supply on a retained section; sidecar audit | Canonical `course_materials` rows for retained material sections only | Never NULL; false means no classified supply |
| `supply_count` | `bigint` | Count of `is_supply` rows in the sidecar | #36: co-occurring classified supply rows on a retained section; sidecar audit | Canonical `course_materials` rows for retained material sections only | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `is_oer` | `boolean` | `BOOL_OR(is_oer)` over `material_costs`, coalesced false | COALESCE(BOOL_OR(is_oer), FALSE) over canonical material_costs items | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; false means no classified OER item |
| `is_ia` | `boolean` | `BOOL_OR(is_ia)` over `material_costs`, coalesced false | TRUE or FALSE | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; false means no classified IA item |
| `oer_count` | `bigint` | Count of canonical items with `is_oer=true` | Non-negative whole-number count | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `ia_count` | `bigint` | Count of canonical items with `is_ia=true` | Non-negative whole-number count | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `publishers` | `varchar[]` | `LIST(DISTINCT publisher)` for non-NULL `material_costs` publishers | LIST(DISTINCT publisher) | Canonical items in the section | No canonical item has a publisher |
| `required_publishers` | `varchar[]` | `LIST(DISTINCT publisher)` where `is_required_inferred` | LIST(DISTINCT publisher) WHERE required | Required canonical items in the section | No required item has a publisher |
| `required_publisher_count` | `bigint` | `COUNT(DISTINCT publisher)` where required | Non-negative whole-number count | Required canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `optional_publisher_count` | `bigint` | `COUNT(DISTINCT publisher)` where not required | Non-negative whole-number count | Optional canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `enrollments` | `integer` | Exact section value from `section_enrollment` | Whole number in the declared integer range | Material-section rows; computed upstream over the full section population | No reported enrollment |
| `seats_taken` | `integer` | Exact section value from `section_enrollment`; raw 9999 sentinel retained | Whole number in the declared integer range | Material-section rows; computed upstream over the full section population | No reported seats value |
| `has_isbn` | `boolean` | `BOOL_OR(has_isbn)` over `material_costs` | BOOL_OR(has_isbn) over canonical items; true under the current Use contract | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; true under the current Use contract |
| `has_formattype` | `boolean` | `BOOL_OR(has_formattype)` over `material_costs` | BOOL_OR(FormatType present) over canonical items; OER/IA classifiability flag | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; false means no nonblank FormatType |
| `isbn_count` | `bigint` | Count of canonical items with `has_isbn`; currently equals `material_count` | canonical ISBN items; currently equals material_count | Canonical section×ISBN items | Never NULL or zero |
| `classified_count` | `bigint` | Count of canonical items with nonblank `FormatType` | canonical items carrying a FormatType | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `has_enrollment` | `boolean` | Exact flag from `section_enrollment` | section carries its own enrollment | Material-section rows | Never NULL |
| `has_enrollment_sibling` | `boolean` | Exact flag from `section_enrollment` | another section of the same course+period has enrollment | Material-section rows; sibling search occurs upstream over the full population | Never NULL |
| `has_enrollment_own_seats` | `boolean` | Exact flag from `section_enrollment` | this section has usable seats_taken (<9999) | Material-section rows | Never NULL |
| `has_enrollment_sibling_seats` | `boolean` | Exact flag from `section_enrollment` | a sibling section has usable seats_taken | Material-section rows; sibling search occurs upstream over the full population | Never NULL |
| `enrollment_assigned` | `integer` | Exact assignment from `section_enrollment`; first available of own enrollment, own seats, sibling medians, control×level median, or level median | #32: filled enrollment. own -> own_seats(<9999) -> sibling_enroll -> sibling_seats -> class_median(control x level) -> level_median. NULL only when enrollment_source=none | Material-section rows; medians use the documented per-term full reference population | No rung produced a value (`enrollment_source='none'`) |
| `enrollment_source` | `varchar` | Exact label from `section_enrollment` | #32: which fill rung supplied enrollment_assigned: own \| own_seats \| sibling_enroll \| sibling_seats \| class_median \| level_median \| none | Material-section rows | Never NULL; `none` means unassigned |
| `required_cost_total_min` | `decimal(38,2)` | Sum of each required Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_min` | SUM of per-material price_min over distinct required materials (from section_cost) | Distinct priced required Use materials in the section | No required Use material has a valid price |
| `required_cost_total_max` | `decimal(38,2)` | Sum of each required Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_max` | USD decimal at the precision declared in DBML | Distinct priced required Use materials in the section | No required Use material has a valid price |
| `optional_cost_total_min` | `decimal(38,2)` | Sum of each optional Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_min` | USD decimal at the precision declared in DBML | Distinct priced optional Use materials in the section | No optional Use material has a valid price |
| `optional_cost_total_max` | `decimal(38,2)` | Sum of each optional Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_max` | USD decimal at the precision declared in DBML | Distinct priced optional Use materials in the section | No optional Use material has a valid price |
| `required_cost_owned_min` | `decimal(38,2)` | Sum of each required Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_buy_min` | buy-only (rentals excluded) | Distinct required Use materials with a buy price | No required Use material has a valid buy price |
| `required_cost_owned_max` | `decimal(38,2)` | Sum of each required Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_buy_max` | USD decimal at the precision declared in DBML | Distinct required Use materials with a buy price | No required Use material has a valid buy price |
| `optional_cost_owned_min` | `decimal(38,2)` | Sum of each optional Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_buy_min` | USD decimal at the precision declared in DBML | Distinct optional Use materials with a buy price | No optional Use material has a valid buy price |
| `optional_cost_owned_max` | `decimal(38,2)` | Sum of each optional Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_buy_max` | USD decimal at the precision declared in DBML | Distinct optional Use materials with a buy price | No optional Use material has a valid buy price |
| `required_cost_avg` | `double` | (`required_cost_total_min` + `required_cost_total_max`) / 2 | (min+max)/2 — legacy price_avg convention, NOT arithmetic mean | Same priced required population as the total bounds | Either total bound is NULL |
| `required_cost_owned_avg` | `double` | (`required_cost_owned_min` + `required_cost_owned_max`) / 2 | USD decimal at the precision declared in DBML | Same buy-priced required population as the owned bounds | Either owned bound is NULL |
| `optional_cost_avg` | `double` | (`optional_cost_total_min` + `optional_cost_total_max`) / 2 | USD decimal at the precision declared in DBML | Same priced optional population as the total bounds | Either total bound is NULL |
| `required_priced_count` | `bigint` | Required canonical ISBN items with non-NULL `price_min` | distinct required materials with ANY price (total coverage; NOT owned-only — can be >0 while owned cost is NULL when priced materials are rental-only) | Required items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none has a valid price |
| `optional_priced_count` | `bigint` | Optional canonical ISBN items with non-NULL `price_min` | Non-negative whole-number count | Optional items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none has a valid price |

- Grain: One material-bearing period × course
- Upstream: `master_section`, `section_cost`

- DBML relation note: One row per (course_id × period_sortable × period × period_date × school × department × course_number × course_title × publisher × book_status), consumed directly from material_costs. SQL filters NULL course_id, publisher, and period_sortable before grouping; other group keys may be NULL.

### `master_section_us_intro_fall2025`

- Kind: view
- Stage: EDA records / 4_merged_records.sql
- Grain: Filtered master_section rows
- Upstream: `master_section`
- Intended use: Fall 2025 required intro/intermediate scope using the executable non-Canada/nonblank-state proxy.
- Projection: All columns inherited from master_section; filtered projection only.
- DBML relation note: BMG #38. Filtered projection of material-bearing master_section (NO new columns, same schema): period_sortable=2025-4, required_count>=1, course_level in {Introductory or general undergraduate, Intermediate undergraduate}, and state NOT IN (CAN, blank); NULL state is also excluded by SQL three-valued logic. This is a non-Canada/nonblank-state proxy, not validated institution-country membership. Current row count: 773,613. Enrichment stays on master_section; this view only projects/filters.

### `master_institution`

- Kind: table
- Stage: Release model / models/master_institution.sql
- Grain: One period × institution, including an explicit NULL-institution bucket
- Upstream: `master_section`, `section_book_status`, `pricing_wide`
- Intended use: Canonical materialized per-term institution release table.
- DBML relation note: Issue #54/#58. One row per term × institution key represented by material-bearing master_section. All section/enrollment/material/pricing denominators use that population. Supply metrics are retained-section sidecar audits. bookstore_url is the explicit metadata exception sourced from all same-term pricing_wide rows.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `period_sortable` | `varchar` | Institution-rollup group key from `master_section.period_sortable`. | YYYY-N; part of canonical key | Material-bearing master-section rows for one `(period_sortable, unit_id)` bucket, including NULL unit_id. | `unit_id` is NULL for the explicit unmatched-institution bucket; period is required. |
| `unit_id` | `bigint` | Institution-rollup group key from `master_section.unit_id`. | IPEDS institution identifier; NULL is one explicit unknown-institution bucket per term | Material-bearing master-section rows for one `(period_sortable, unit_id)` bucket, including NULL unit_id. | `unit_id` is NULL for the explicit unmatched-institution bucket; period is required. |
| `state` | `varchar` | `ANY_VALUE(master_section.state)`; IPEDS attributes are institution-grain. | State text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Material-bearing master-section rows for one `(period_sortable, unit_id)` bucket, including NULL unit_id. | No matching IPEDS/source institution value. |
| `control` | `varchar` | `ANY_VALUE(master_section.control)`; IPEDS attributes are institution-grain. | Control text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | Material-bearing master-section rows for one `(period_sortable, unit_id)` bucket, including NULL unit_id. | No matching IPEDS/source institution value. |

| `distance_enrollment_2024` | `integer` | `ANY_VALUE(master_section.distance_enrollment_2024)`; IPEDS attributes are institution-grain. | Whole number in the declared integer range | Material-bearing master-section rows for one `(period_sortable, unit_id)` bucket, including NULL unit_id. | No matching IPEDS/source institution value. |

This institution-level metadata exception remains a compatibility path. | All same-term pricing rows for the institution, not only canonical Use items. | No nonblank pricing URL, or unit_id is NULL. |

### `master_isbn`

- Kind: table
- Stage: Release model / models/master_isbn.sql
- Grain: One period × non-NULL ISBN
- Upstream: `material_costs`
- Intended use: Canonical materialized per-term ISBN release table.
- DBML relation note: Issue #55/#58. One row per term × ISBN consumed from material_costs; Canada, blank ISBN, supply, no-details, and no-material rows are excluded. Pseudo-SKU access codes remain eligible unless classified supply. Price counts are distinct sections with non-NULL material_costs cells.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `period_sortable` | `varchar` | Group key from `material_costs.period_sortable`. | YYYY-N; part of canonical key | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Not produced. |
| `isbn13` | `bigint` | Group key from `material_costs.isbn13`. | Non-NULL ISBN; part of canonical key; ISBN or source identifier/pseudo-SKU represented in the declared relation type | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Not produced. |
| `book_title` | `varchar` | `ANY_VALUE(material_costs.isbn_book_title)`; term×ISBN metadata is canonical upstream. | Lexical MIN of trimmed nonblank catalog values | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | No nonmissing canonical ISBN metadata. |
| `author` | `varchar` | `ANY_VALUE(material_costs.isbn_author)`; term×ISBN metadata is canonical upstream. | Lexical MIN of trimmed nonblank catalog values | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | No nonmissing canonical ISBN metadata. |
| `publisher` | `varchar` | `ANY_VALUE(material_costs.isbn_publisher)`; term×ISBN metadata is canonical upstream. | Lexical MIN of trimmed nonblank catalog values | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | No nonmissing canonical ISBN metadata. |
| `is_oer` | `boolean` | `COALESCE(BOOL_OR(material_costs.is_oer), FALSE)`. | Definitive BOOL_OR across catalog rows | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Never NULL; false means no canonical ISBN row has the classification. |
| `is_ia` | `boolean` | `COALESCE(BOOL_OR(material_costs.is_ia), FALSE)`. | Definitive BOOL_OR across catalog rows | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Never NULL; false means no canonical ISBN row has the classification. |
| `is_supply` | `boolean` | Constant FALSE: supplies are excluded from the canonical Use population. | Always false in the canonical Use population | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Never NULL. |
| `unit_id_count` | `bigint` | `COUNT(DISTINCT unit_id)`. | Non-negative whole-number count | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Never NULL; zero means all values of that key are missing. |
| `section_id_count` | `bigint` | `COUNT(DISTINCT section_id)`. | Non-negative whole-number count | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Never NULL; zero means all values of that key are missing. |
| `course_id_count` | `bigint` | `COUNT(DISTINCT course_id)`. | Non-negative whole-number count | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Never NULL; zero means all values of that key are missing. |
| `has_enrollment` | `boolean` | `COALESCE(BOOL_OR(enrollment_assigned IS NOT NULL), FALSE)`. | TRUE or FALSE | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Never NULL; false means no occurrence has assigned enrollment. |
| `enroll_cnt` | `bigint` | `COUNT(DISTINCT section_id) FILTER (WHERE enrollment_assigned IS NOT NULL)`. | Distinct sections with enrollment_assigned | Distinct ISBN-using sections. | Never NULL; zero means none has assigned enrollment. |
| `enroll_tot` | `hugeint` | `COALESCE(SUM(enrollment_assigned), 0)` across ISBN occurrences. | SUM(enrollment_assigned) over the distinct section × ISBN spine | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Never NULL; zero means no assigned enrollment or assigned total zero. |
| `title_variant_count` | `bigint` | `ANY_VALUE(material_costs.isbn_title_variant_count)`. | Non-negative whole-number count | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Not expected; upstream count is non-NULL. |
| `author_variant_count` | `bigint` | `ANY_VALUE(material_costs.isbn_author_variant_count)`. | Non-negative whole-number count | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Not expected; upstream count is non-NULL. |
| `publisher_variant_count` | `bigint` | `ANY_VALUE(material_costs.isbn_publisher_variant_count)`. | Non-negative whole-number count | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Not expected; upstream count is non-NULL. |
| `metadata_conflict` | `boolean` | True when any title/author/publisher variant count exceeds one. | True when any metadata variant count exceeds one | Canonical Use `material_costs` rows for one term×non-NULL ISBN. | Never NULL; false means no detected canonical metadata conflict. |
| `price_buy_new_physical_count` | `bigint` | `COUNT(DISTINCT section_id) FILTER (WHERE price_buy_new_physical IS NOT NULL)`. | Non-negative whole-number count | Distinct ISBN-using sections in the term. | Never NULL; zero means no section has that price cell. |
| `price_buy_new_digital_count` | `bigint` | `COUNT(DISTINCT section_id) FILTER (WHERE price_buy_new_digital IS NOT NULL)`. | Non-negative whole-number count | Distinct ISBN-using sections in the term. | Never NULL; zero means no section has that price cell. |
| `price_buy_new_na_count` | `bigint` | `COUNT(DISTINCT section_id) FILTER (WHERE price_buy_new_na IS NOT NULL)`. | Non-negative whole-number count | Distinct ISBN-using sections in the term. | Never NULL; zero means no section has that price cell. |
| `price_buy_used_physical_count` | `bigint` | `COUNT(DISTINCT section_id) FILTER (WHERE price_buy_used_physical IS NOT NULL)`. | Non-negative whole-number count | Distinct ISBN-using sections in the term. | Never NULL; zero means no section has that price cell. |

### `sample10_section_ids`

- Kind: table
- Stage: Sampling / models/sample10_section_ids.sql
- Grain: One selected section_enrollment section
- Upstream: `section_enrollment`
- Intended use: Stable deterministic 10% section-membership lookup.
- DBML relation note: Issue #53. Deterministic md5-prefix64-mod10-v1 membership over the complete section_enrollment population: 2,359,278 current members with the preserved fingerprint. All sampled stages join this table so whole section clusters and identical membership are preserved; the current Master Section intersection is 698,578 rows.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `section_id` | `varchar` | Distinct `section_enrollment.section_id` group key. | Canonical sampling unit; one selected row per full section_enrollment key | Distinct complete-population section IDs. | Not produced. |
| `period_sortable` | `varchar` | `ANY_VALUE(section_enrollment.period_sortable)` for the section. | `YYYY-N`; 1=Winter, 2=Spring, 3=Summer, 4=Fall | Distinct complete-population section IDs. | Not expected for retained section_enrollment rows. |
| `section_hash` | `ubigint` | Unsigned integer from `CAST('0x' \|\| LEFT(md5(section_id),16) AS UBIGINT)`. | First 64 bits of MD5(section_id), interpreted unsigned | Distinct complete-population section IDs. | Not produced. |
| `sample_bucket` | `utinyint` | `section_hash % 10`, cast to UTINYINT. | section_hash modulo 10; always 0 in this membership table | Distinct complete-population section IDs. | Not produced. |

### `__data_quality_metrics`

- Kind: table
- Stage: Data quality / 2d_data_quality.sql
- Grain: One category × check × metric
- Upstream: `course_catalog_20251215`, `comprehensive_data`, `pricing_historical`, `pricing_wide`, `BMG BookPricing.Historical_20260224.csv`
- Intended use: Long-format pipeline quality metrics, including direct raw-pricing deduplication checks.
- DBML relation note: Long-format scalar metrics. Recreated on each pipeline run.

| Column name | Data type | Source / derivation | Values / format | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `category` | `varchar` | Literal metric family emitted by `2d_data_quality.sql`. | catalog \| pricing \| wide \| oer_ia \| cross_table | One row per emitted category×check×metric in the current pipeline snapshot. | Metric keys are not NULL; metric_value can be NULL only when its SQL aggregate has no contributing rows. |
| `check_id` | `varchar` | Literal executable check identifier emitted by `2d_data_quality.sql`. | short identifier, e.g., dedupe_stages, price_outliers | One row per emitted category×check×metric in the current pipeline snapshot. | Metric keys are not NULL; metric_value can be NULL only when its SQL aggregate has no contributing rows. |
| `metric_name` | `varchar` | Literal scalar measure name emitted by `2d_data_quality.sql`. | Metric name text; spelling/casing follows the stated source or derivation | One row per emitted category×check×metric in the current pipeline snapshot. | Metric keys are not NULL; metric_value can be NULL only when its SQL aggregate has no contributing rows. |
| `metric_value` | `bigint` | BIGINT scalar computed by the named check; scope varies by category/check_id and is preserved by those keys. | Non-negative whole-number count | One row per emitted category×check×metric in the current pipeline snapshot. | Metric keys are not NULL; metric_value can be NULL only when its SQL aggregate has no contributing rows. |

| `total_null_isbn` | `bigint` | COUNT(*) of NULL-ISBN rows in `2d_data_quality.sql`. | Non-negative whole-number count | Required-inferred catalog rows with NULL ISBN; top 20 schools. | Never NULL for an emitted diagnostic row. |

## Detailed Master Section appendix

The following repo-level contract is embedded from `MASTER-SECTION-DICTIONARY.md`
so the synced Notion Data Dictionary remains self-contained.

This repo-level appendix is the detailed business, NULL, population, and denominator contract for
the release-facing `master_section` table. The generated project-wide `DATA-DICTIONARY.md` embeds
this appendix so the synced Data Dictionary page remains self-contained. Its grain is one
row per distinct `(period_sortable, section_id)` represented by canonical `material_costs` for
2024+ terms. “Material-section population” below means those sections; the independent
`section_enrollment` table retains the complete valid section population, including no-ISBN and
no-adoption sections. “Use” means the issue-#58 material population documented in `CMM-ETL.md`.

DuckDB reports every materialized-table column as nullable at the schema level. The NULL column
below describes the semantic contract produced by the SQL, rather than that generic catalog
metadata. Counts and definitive booleans are non-NULL unless explicitly noted.

### Identity, period, institution, and course descriptors

| Column | Business label | Source / derivation | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `section_id` | Section-offering ID | Group key from `material_costs`; includes term | Material-section population | Not produced |
| `course_id` | Course ID | Exact section value from `section_enrollment`; source composite omits section and term | Material-section population | Not expected; missing source segments are encoded as `UNKNOWN` |
| `period` | Academic period | `ANY_VALUE(period)` from `material_costs` | Material-section population | Not expected because valid sortable periods are required |
| `period_sortable` | Sortable term | Group key from `material_costs`; `YYYY-N` | Material-section population | Not produced |
| `period_date` | Canonical term date | `ANY_VALUE(period_date)` from `material_costs` | Material-section population, restricted to 2024+ | Not produced by the retained scope |
| `unit_id` | IPEDS institution ID | `ANY_VALUE(unit_id)` from `material_costs` | Material-section population | Source institution ID missing |
| `state` | State/province code | `ANY_VALUE(state)` from `material_costs` | Material-section population | No source geography |
| `control` | Institution control | Exact section value from `section_enrollment` | Material-section population | No matching IPEDS institution |
| `level` | Institution level | Exact section value from `section_enrollment` | Material-section population | No matching IPEDS institution |
| `size` | Institution size band | `ANY_VALUE(size)` from `material_costs` | Material-section population | No matching IPEDS institution |
| `sector` | IPEDS sector | Exact section value from `section_enrollment` | Material-section population | No matching IPEDS institution |
| `institution_name` | Institution name | `ANY_VALUE(institution_name)` from `material_costs` | Material-section population | No matching IPEDS institution |
| `institution_type` | Institution type | `ANY_VALUE(institution_type)` from `material_costs` | Material-section population | No matching IPEDS institution/type |
| `enrollment_2024` | Institution enrollment, 2024 | `ANY_VALUE(enrollment_2024)` from `material_costs` | Material-section population | No matching IPEDS value |
| `distance_enrollment_2024` | Institution distance enrollment, 2024 | `ANY_VALUE(distance_enrollment_2024)` from `material_costs` | Material-section population | No matching IPEDS value |
| `school` | School/college | Most-frequent non-NULL `material_costs` value via `mode()` | Canonical items in the section | No non-NULL source value |
| `department` | Department | Most-frequent non-NULL `material_costs` value via `mode()` | Canonical items in the section | No non-NULL source value |
| `course_number` | Course number | Most-frequent non-NULL `material_costs` value via `mode()` | Canonical items in the section | No non-NULL source value |
| `section` | Source section code | Most-frequent non-NULL `material_costs` value via `mode()` | Canonical items in the section | No non-NULL source value |
| `course_title` | Course title | Most-frequent non-NULL `material_costs` value via `mode()` | Canonical items in the section | No non-NULL source value |
| `course_level` | BMG course level | Exact section value from `section_enrollment` | Material-section population | No non-NULL source value |
| `course_subject` | Course subject | Most-frequent non-NULL `material_costs` value via `mode()` | Canonical items in the section | No non-NULL source value |

`mode()` intentionally flattens occasional within-section source conflicts. The pipeline logs the
number of divergent sections separately for each descriptor; it does not silently deduplicate the
underlying catalog rows.

### Material population, classification, and publishers

| Column | Business label | Source / derivation | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `material_count` | Course-material item count | `COUNT(*)` over deduplicated `material_costs` | Canonical section×ISBN items | Never NULL or zero |
| `required_count` | Required material item count | Count of `material_costs` rows where `is_required_inferred` | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `optional_count` | Optional/supplemental material item count | Count of `material_costs` rows where not `is_required_inferred` | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `has_course_material_use` | Has an included material | Constant true for a retained material section | Material-section population | Never NULL or false |
| `course_material_use_count` | Included material audit count | Count of canonical items; equals `material_count` | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL or zero |
| `course_material_no_use_count` | Co-occurring excluded-row audit | Count of `course_materials.is_course_material_no_use` rows | Canonical rows for retained material sections only | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `no_details_count` | Co-occurring no-book-details audit | Count of exact `*No Book Details*` rows in the sidecar | Canonical `course_materials` rows for retained material sections only; reasons may overlap | Never NULL; zero means marker absent |
| `no_materials_count` | Co-occurring no-material audit | Count of exact `*No Books Required*` or `placeholder_no_material` rows in the sidecar | Canonical `course_materials` rows for retained material sections only; reasons may overlap | Never NULL; zero means marker absent |
| `is_canada` | Co-occurring Canadian-row indicator | `BOOL_OR(state='CAN')` in the sidecar | Canonical `course_materials` rows for retained material sections only | Never NULL; false means no Canadian row |
| `is_supply` | Has a co-occurring classified supply | `BOOL_OR(is_supply)` in the sidecar | Canonical `course_materials` rows for retained material sections only | Never NULL; false means no classified supply |
| `supply_count` | Co-occurring classified supply row count | Count of `is_supply` rows in the sidecar | Canonical `course_materials` rows for retained material sections only | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `is_oer` | Has OER material | `BOOL_OR(is_oer)` over `material_costs`, coalesced false | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; false means no classified OER item |
| `is_ia` | Has inclusive-access material | `BOOL_OR(is_ia)` over `material_costs`, coalesced false | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; false means no classified IA item |
| `oer_count` | OER material item count | Count of canonical items with `is_oer=true` | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `ia_count` | Inclusive-access material item count | Count of canonical items with `is_ia=true` | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `publishers` | Distinct material publishers | `LIST(DISTINCT publisher)` for non-NULL `material_costs` publishers | Canonical items in the section | No canonical item has a publisher |
| `required_publishers` | Distinct required publishers | `LIST(DISTINCT publisher)` where `is_required_inferred` | Required canonical items in the section | No required item has a publisher |
| `required_publisher_count` | Required publisher count | `COUNT(DISTINCT publisher)` where required | Required canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `optional_publisher_count` | Optional publisher count | `COUNT(DISTINCT publisher)` where not required | Optional canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |
| `has_isbn` | Has an included ISBN | `BOOL_OR(has_isbn)` over `material_costs` | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; true under the current Use contract |
| `has_formattype` | Has a classifiable material | `BOOL_OR(has_formattype)` over `material_costs` | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; false means no nonblank FormatType |
| `isbn_count` | ISBN-bearing material item count | Count of canonical items with `has_isbn`; currently equals `material_count` | Canonical section×ISBN items | Never NULL or zero |
| `classified_count` | FormatType-classifiable material item count | Count of canonical items with nonblank `FormatType` | Canonical items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none |

`required_count + optional_count = material_count = course_material_use_count` is a checked
invariant. Supply, Canada, NoUse, and placeholder counts are sidecar evidence only for retained
sections; use `comprehensive_data` or `section_enrollment` for complete-population analysis.
The validated canonical sidecar after the #65 rebuild totals
`course_material_no_use_count=78,230`, `no_details_count=25,993`,
`no_materials_count=145`, and `supply_count=52,237`; these replace the old raw-row comparisons
for the retained material-bearing sections.

### Enrollment and fill provenance

| Column | Business label | Source / derivation | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `enrollments` | Reported section enrollment | Exact section value from `section_enrollment` | Material-section rows; computed upstream over the full section population | No reported enrollment |
| `seats_taken` | Reported seats taken | Exact section value from `section_enrollment`; raw 9999 sentinel retained | Material-section rows; computed upstream over the full section population | No reported seats value |
| `has_enrollment` | Has own enrollment | Exact flag from `section_enrollment` | Material-section rows | Never NULL |
| `has_enrollment_sibling` | Sibling has enrollment | Exact flag from `section_enrollment` | Material-section rows; sibling search occurs upstream over the full population | Never NULL |
| `has_enrollment_own_seats` | Has usable own seats | Exact flag from `section_enrollment` | Material-section rows | Never NULL |
| `has_enrollment_sibling_seats` | Sibling has usable seats | Exact flag from `section_enrollment` | Material-section rows; sibling search occurs upstream over the full population | Never NULL |
| `enrollment_assigned` | Assigned section enrollment | Exact assignment from `section_enrollment`; first available of own enrollment, own seats, sibling medians, control×level median, or level median | Material-section rows; medians use the documented per-term full reference population | No rung produced a value (`enrollment_source='none'`) |
| `enrollment_source` | Enrollment provenance | Exact label from `section_enrollment` | Material-section rows | Never NULL; `none` means unassigned |

Valid `enrollment_source` values are `own`, `own_seats`, `sibling_enroll`, `sibling_seats`,
`class_median`, `level_median`, and `none`. Raw enrollment fields are never overwritten.

### Cost and price coverage

Cost fields come from `section_cost`, which aggregates canonical `(period_sortable, section_id,
isbn13)` Use items from `material_costs`. A material contributes only when the named pricing bound exists; missing prices are not
treated as zero. “Owned” means buy-only and excludes rental cells. “Average” is the legacy midpoint,
not an arithmetic mean.

| Column | Business label | Source / derivation | Population / denominator | NULL meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `required_cost_total_min` | Required cost, all-options minimum | Sum of each required Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_min` | Distinct priced required Use materials in the section | No required Use material has a valid price |
| `required_cost_total_max` | Required cost, all-options maximum | Sum of each required Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_max` | Distinct priced required Use materials in the section | No required Use material has a valid price |
| `optional_cost_total_min` | Optional cost, all-options minimum | Sum of each optional Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_min` | Distinct priced optional Use materials in the section | No optional Use material has a valid price |
| `optional_cost_total_max` | Optional cost, all-options maximum | Sum of each optional Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_max` | Distinct priced optional Use materials in the section | No optional Use material has a valid price |
| `required_cost_owned_min` | Required buy-only minimum | Sum of each required Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_buy_min` | Distinct required Use materials with a buy price | No required Use material has a valid buy price |
| `required_cost_owned_max` | Required buy-only maximum | Sum of each required Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_buy_max` | Distinct required Use materials with a buy price | No required Use material has a valid buy price |
| `optional_cost_owned_min` | Optional buy-only minimum | Sum of each optional Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_buy_min` | Distinct optional Use materials with a buy price | No optional Use material has a valid buy price |
| `optional_cost_owned_max` | Optional buy-only maximum | Sum of each optional Use ISBN's `material_costs.price_buy_max` | Distinct optional Use materials with a buy price | No optional Use material has a valid buy price |
| `required_cost_avg` | Required all-options midpoint | (`required_cost_total_min` + `required_cost_total_max`) / 2 | Same priced required population as the total bounds | Either total bound is NULL |
| `required_cost_owned_avg` | Required buy-only midpoint | (`required_cost_owned_min` + `required_cost_owned_max`) / 2 | Same buy-priced required population as the owned bounds | Either owned bound is NULL |
| `optional_cost_avg` | Optional all-options midpoint | (`optional_cost_total_min` + `optional_cost_total_max`) / 2 | Same priced optional population as the total bounds | Either total bound is NULL |
| `required_priced_count` | Required priced material count | Required canonical ISBN items with non-NULL `price_min` | Required items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none has a valid price |
| `optional_priced_count` | Optional priced material count | Optional canonical ISBN items with non-NULL `price_min` | Optional items in the section | Never NULL; zero means none has a valid price |

### Release and validation

`scripts/export_cmm_masters.sh` is the sole per-term release splitter. It selects every column from
the canonical materialized table, filters only `period_sortable`, orders by `section_id`, and writes
`master_section_<YYYY_N>_<YYYYMMDD>.csv`. It does not reimplement any population or aggregation.

The executable DQ in `scripts/sql/4_merged_records.sql` checks section-key uniqueness, encoded-term
agreement, exact `material_costs` section-key conservation, required/optional partitioning,
boolean/count agreement, cost bounds, and retained-section sidecar invariants. Full-population
checks remain on `comprehensive_data`/`section_enrollment`; canonical item checks use
`course_materials`. Cross-model and deterministic sample
reconciliation is in `scripts/sql/exports/37_sample10_reconciliation.sql`; exact current-state
release and material-section key-set reconciliation is in
`scripts/sql/exports/38_cmm_release_reconciliation.sql` and
`39_cmm_release_key_reconciliation.sql`.
